## PLG-007..009 (Filed 2026-07-07, Codex-Maintenance — re-filed after concurrent-agent overwrite)

- [ ] **[PLG-007]** [Threading] Sync blocking in UI click handler at ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs:294. *Root Cause*: _plugin.SyncManagedConfigurationAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult() called directly from a UI button click handler. Currently safe because the underlying method completes synchronously (zero wait expressions), but adding any wait inside SyncManagedConfigurationAsync would immediately deadlock the UI thread. This is a maintenance trap. *Suggested Fix*: wait _plugin.SyncManagedConfigurationAsync(); (make the click handler sync void and await instead of blocking).

- [ ] **[PLG-008]** [Threading] Sync wrappers with GetAwaiter().GetResult() in ShellIntegrationPlugin.cs:306,320,357. *Root Cause*: Three synchronous wrapper methods call SyncManagedConfigurationAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult(). Same maintenance trap as PLG-007: the method happens to complete synchronously today, but any future wait addition inside creates a deadlock. The sync wrappers themselves are the root problem. *Suggested Fix*: Remove sync wrappers; have callers await the async method directly, or use Task.Run(async => ...).GetAwaiter().GetResult() as a deadlock-safe fallback if sync API surface is required.

- [ ] **[PLG-009]** [Exception Handling] Empty catch blocks in WpfHostNotifications.cs:41,67. *Root Cause*: catch { /* ignore */ } at L41 and catch { /* try next */ } at L67 silently swallow all exceptions including OutOfMemoryException, StackOverflowException, etc. No logging, no telemetry. Failed notification attempts are invisible. *Suggested Fix*: Replace with catch (Exception ex) when (ex is not (OutOfMemoryException or StackOverflowException)) and add Log.Instance.Trace("Notification attempt failed", ex).
